<script>
  (function() {
    var jdgmThemeFixes = null;
    if (!jdgmThemeFixes) return;

    // "All themes" fix: CSS and JS applied on every theme. CSS is injected
    // before the per-theme fix so the theme-specific CSS wins the cascade.
    // HTML is never applied globally (too risky).
    var allThemesFix = jdgmThemeFixes['all'];
    if (allThemesFix && allThemesFix.css) {
      var allStyleTag = document.createElement('style');
      allStyleTag.classList.add('jdgm-theme-fix-style-all');
      allStyleTag.innerHTML = allThemesFix.css;
      document.head.append(allStyleTag);
    };

    if (allThemesFix && allThemesFix.js) {
      var allScriptTag = document.createElement('script');
      allScriptTag.classList.add('jdgm-theme-fix-script-all');
      allScriptTag.innerHTML = allThemesFix.js;
      document.head.append(allScriptTag);
    };

    var thisThemeFix = jdgmThemeFixes[Shopify.theme.id];
    if (!thisThemeFix) return;

    if (thisThemeFix.html) {
      document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", function() {
        var htmlDiv = document.createElement('div');
        htmlDiv.classList.add('jdgm-theme-fix-html');
        htmlDiv.innerHTML = thisThemeFix.html;
        document.body.append(htmlDiv);
      });
    };

    if (thisThemeFix.css) {
      var styleTag = document.createElement('style');
      styleTag.classList.add('jdgm-theme-fix-style');
      styleTag.innerHTML = thisThemeFix.css;
      document.head.append(styleTag);
    };

    if (thisThemeFix.js) {
      var scriptTag = document.createElement('script');
      scriptTag.classList.add('jdgm-theme-fix-script');
      scriptTag.innerHTML = thisThemeFix.js;
      document.head.append(scriptTag);
    };
  })();
</script>

  <script>
    function toggleFeaturesMask(blockId, isExpand) {
      const container = document.getElementById('block-' + blockId);
      const outer = container.querySelector('.description-wrapper-outer');
      const inner = container.querySelector('.description-content-inner');
      const moreBtn = container.querySelector('.button-more');
      const lessBtn = container.querySelector('.button-less');

      if (isExpand) {
        container.setAttribute('data-expanded', 'true');
        outer.style.maxHeight = (inner.scrollHeight + 2) + 'px';
        moreBtn.style.display = 'none';
        lessBtn.style.display = 'inline-flex';
      } else {
        container.setAttribute('data-expanded', 'false');
        outer.style.maxHeight = '4.6em'; 
        moreBtn.style.display = 'inline-flex';
        lessBtn.style.display = 'none';
      }
    }
  </script>

                  <h6><strong>Sweat-Proof PCB Coating: Enhanced Protection Against Sweat and Humidity</strong></h6><p>We have applied an additional moisture-resistant coating to the circuit board of the mouse. This coating assists in shielding core circuitry from sweat, humidity, accidental splashes, and minor dust intrusion, ensuring greater stability and long-lasting durability.</p><h6><strong>Accidental Water Exposure – Immediate Action Required</strong></h6><p>The PCB coating is designed for short-term protection, not as a permanent barrier. Upon accidental water exposure, please disconnect the power immediately, wipe off any surface fluids, and blow-dry it thoroughly with cool air or let it air dry completely. Ensure the internal components are completely dry before using the mouse again. Do not operate the mouse while it is wet, to prevent residual moisture from damaging the circuitry.</p><h6><strong>Liquid Damage – Not Covered by Warranty</strong></h6><p>The mouse is not a fully sealed structure. There are openings necessary for normal operation around the sensor, micro-switches, and other components.Any liquid (including water, beverages, sweat, etc.) that enters the mouse through these openings is considered liquid ingress. Regardless of the volume of liquid, any malfunctions, damage, or operational issues resulting from it are not covered under the product's warranty. Please exercise care and protect your device during daily use.</p><p><strong>For Customers from Korea, Indonesia, Argentina:</strong></p><p>Please provide your <strong>PCC, NPWP,</strong> or <strong>CUIT</strong> as required by your country’s regulations. <script>
  (() => {
    const section = document.getElementById('shopify-section-template--28082621743328__video_gallery_custom_TFc6Ci');
    if (!section || section.dataset.videoGalleryReady === 'true') return;

    section.dataset.videoGalleryReady = 'true';
    const loadVideo = (video) => {
      if (video.dataset.loaded === 'true') return;

      video.querySelectorAll('source[data-src]').forEach((source) => {
        source.src = source.dataset.src;
        source.removeAttribute('data-src');
      });
      video.dataset.loaded = 'true';
      video.load();
    };

    const lazyVideos = section.querySelectorAll('[data-video-gallery-lazy]');
    if ('IntersectionObserver' in window) {
      const observer = new IntersectionObserver((entries, activeObserver) => {
        entries.forEach((entry) => {
          if (entry.isIntersecting) {
            loadVideo(entry.target);
            activeObserver.unobserve(entry.target);
          }
        });
      }, { rootMargin: '400px 0px' });

      lazyVideos.forEach((video) => observer.observe(video));
    } else {
      lazyVideos.forEach(loadVideo);
    }

  })();
</script>

It’s a great mouse. But for many of us, the full-size profile is just slightly too big for precise micro-adjustments.</p><p>We’ve analyzed the feedback, and came to the conclusion that the issue isn't just simply that the mouse is "too big". The issue is that the excess length at the back hits your palm when you pull down for recoil control. You are fighting the shape to find space for micro-adjustments.</p></div></div>

<script>
function toggleSpecs() {
  const box = document.getElementById("specsBox");
  const btn = document.getElementById("toggleBtn");

  box.classList.toggle("expanded");

  if (box.classList.contains("expanded")) {
    btn.innerHTML = "Collapse ↑";
  } else {
    btn.innerHTML = "View More ↓";
  }
}
</script>

data-product-url='https://store.angrymiao.com/products/am-infinity-97-wireless-gaming-mouse' data-thumb-up-count='0' data-thumb-down-count='0'> <div class='jdgm-rev__header'>  <div class='jdgm-rev__icon' > D </div>  <span class='jdgm-rev__rating' data-score='5' tabindex='0' aria-label='5 star review' role='img'> <span class='jdgm-star jdgm--on'></span><span class='jdgm-star jdgm--on'></span><span class='jdgm-star jdgm--on'></span><span class='jdgm-star jdgm--on'></span><span class='jdgm-star jdgm--on'></span> </span> <time class='jdgm-rev__timestamp jdgm-spinner' datetime='2026-08-10T19:29:53Z' data-content='2026-08-10 19:29:53 UTC'></time>    <div class='jdgm-rev__br'></div> <span class='jdgm-rev__buyer-badge-wrapper'> <span class='jdgm-rev__buyer-badge'></span> </span> <span class='jdgm-rev__author-wrapper'> <span class='jdgm-rev__author'>David Smith</span> <span class='jdgm-rev__location'  data-country-code="US" > (Denton, US) </span>  </span> </div> <div class='jdgm-rev__content'>  <div class='jdgm-rev__custom-form'>  </div> <b class='jdgm-rev__title'>A sexy, lightweight, fast, and responsive mouse</b> <div class='jdgm-rev__body'><p>The review title pretty much covers it. I returned the 1.0 version of this to Amazon so that I could get the .97 version (that they don't sell) that can use the hatchback. I got the purple hatchback for mine, which also seems to make up for that .03 weight difference. (Don't want the mouse floating away, now do we?) I noticed a lot of people in reviews are swapping the battery the slow way. If done correctly, using both hands at once, and dropping the batteries down onto the mouse pad and letting the strong magnets reattach the batteries to the mouse and receiver simply by hovering them over the batteries, the swap-out only takes about 5 seconds. Don't waste time turning the mouse upside down and picking up the battery to insert it. I would highly recommend this mouse to any of my friends who enjoy high-speed gaming and fine-detail mouse control. If you use this mouse with a dock, make sure you plug the receiver into a port that provides USB power at all times (usually has a lightning bolt symbol to denote this), so the backup battery can stay charged and ready. I have had zero issues with this mouse so far. I saw a reviewer on Amazon say that the mouse buttons don't react well to being dropped, but honestly, you shouldn't be getting a quality mouse like this and then handling it in a way that could (function () {
  document.addEventListener('click', function (event) {
    var target = event.target.closest('a');
    if (!target) return;

    var href = target.href || '';
    var rawText = target.innerText || target.textContent || '';
    var text = rawText.trim() || (target.getAttribute('aria-label') || '').trim();
    var section = target.closest('[id*="slideshow_homepage_Uk6j7k"]');

    var isAuctionBanner =
      section &&
      href.indexOf('/pages/am-back-to-school-event') !== -1 &&
      text.toLowerCase() === 'bid now';

    var isTaggedAuctionClick =
      target.closest('[data-ga-event="auction_cta_click"], .js-auction-homepage-banner');

    if (!isAuctionBanner && !isTaggedAuctionClick) return;

    var payload = {
      campaign_name: 'back_to_school_auction_2026',
      button_name: target.dataset.gaButtonName || 'bid_now',
      button_location: target.dataset.gaButtonLocation || 'homepage_slideshow',
      button_text: target.dataset.gaButtonText || text || 'Bid Now',
      destination_url: href,
      transport_type: 'beacon'
    };

    if (typeof gtag === 'function') {
      gtag('event', 'auction_cta_click', payload);
    } else {
      window.dataLayer = window.dataLayer || [];
      window.dataLayer.push({
        event: 'auction_cta_click',
        ...payload
      });
    }
  });
})();
</script>
    <script>
  (function () {
    function pushCampaignEvent(eventName, payload) {
      if (typeof window.gtag === 'function') {
        window.gtag('event', eventName, payload);
        return;
      }

      window.dataLayer = window.dataLayer || [];
      window.dataLayer.push(Object.assign({ event: eventName }, payload));
    }

    document.addEventListener('click', function (event) {
      var target = event.target.closest('[data-campaign-event]');
      if (!target) return;

      pushCampaignEvent(target.dataset.campaignEvent || 'campaign_interaction', {
        campaign_name: target.dataset.campaignName || '',
        campaign_action: target.dataset.campaignAction || '',
        campaign_location: target.dataset.campaignLocation || '',
        campaign_label: target.dataset.campaignLabel || (target.innerText || '').trim(),
        destination_url: target.href || (event.target.closest('a') && event.target.closest('a').href) || '',
        page_path: window.location.pathname,
        page_title: document.title,
        transport_type: 'beacon'
      });
    });
  })();
</script>

    <script>
      (function () {
        // globalWidgets must NOT stay NEED_TO_LOAD if it fails (error) OR never
        // settles (hang) — base waits on it in GFG_DEPENDENCIES, so it would block
        // ALL discount modules. A once-only settle() flips it to DONT_LOAD and
        // re-fires gfg:module:loaded so the base dependency waiter proceeds. On a
        // successful load the script sets its own status; we only clear the timeout.
        var settled = false;
        var timer;
        function settle(failed) {
          if (settled) return;
          settled = true;
          if (timer) { clearTimeout(timer); timer = null; }
          if (failed) {
            if (window.gfgFileStatus) { window.gfgFileStatus.globalWidgets = 'DONT_LOAD'; }
            document.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent('gfg:module:loaded', { detail: { moduleName: 'globalWidgets' } }));
          }
        }
        var s = document.createElement('script');
        s.async = true;
        s.src = "https:\/\/cdn.shopify.com\/extensions\/01a19c34-0c85-7674-a9a2-da985370a828\/free-gift-28-827\/assets\/globalWidgets.js";
        s.addEventListener('load', function () { settle(false); });
        s.addEventListener('error', function () { settle(true); });
        timer = setTimeout(function () { settle(true); }, 15000);
        document.head.appendChild(s);
      })();
    </script>
